| Name | Who they were in Chapter 14 |

| **Jayson Goodwin / Shel’mekai’Vuran-Tah’aan** | The player character. Nine-tailed Kitsune, Axiomatic student, Pip’s adoptive father, former godlike being from another universe, and the person who uncovered the university vivarium conspiracy. |

Her university room was later discovered to have been deliberately used by the conspiracy because they assumed a Beastfolk resident would not be believed. She temporarily moved into Room 13. |

| **Lecturer Halven** | University lecturer connected to the conspiracy and the hidden cache in Sari’s room. Jayson baited him into incriminating himself, then subdued, tied, gagged, and turned him over to Aureliane. |

| **Varr** | One of the people involved in the earlier dangerous-animal/channel investigation. Jayson discussed assisting through scent work and later proposed mapping drains, pipes, and connected facilities instead of entering the dangerous channels himself. |
| **Sela** | Another person involved in that same investigation. She was part of the group Jayson discussed procedures and permissions with. |
| **Vaska** | Scarred Orcish registrar at the Guilded Path hall in Valenhold. She helped arrange the portal journey toward Sari’s village and openly shut down racist treatment of Sari. |
| **Fellan** | Human Guilded Path worker who insulted Sari and Mela, referring to Sari as a stray and treating Mela contemptuously. Jayson confronted him verbally. |
| **Borru** | Minotaur portal attendant at the Guilded Path gate. He handled the storm-damaged portal route toward Kharveth Hollow. |
| **Rhuva** | Sari’s mother and Mela’s grandmother. A formidable, affectionate Wolfkin woman and enthusiastic cook. She welcomed Sari home, accepted Mela immediately, and fed Jayson enough food to threaten local agriculture. |
| **Dhorrek** | Sari’s father and Mela’s grandfather. Broad, quiet Wolfkin timber worker/woodworker with old scars and troublesome knees. He strongly approved of Jayson protecting Sari and Mela. |
| **Keshran** | Sari’s older brother and Mela’s uncle. A teasing Wolfkin trail-runner/patrolman with a scar from fighting raiders as a teenager. Quickly adopted the role of troublesome uncle. |
| **Tovrek** | Sari’s neighbour in her foothill village. He loudly announced Sari’s return and appears to function as one of the village’s highly efficient gossip distribution systems. |
| **Veskara** | Vulpanii tailor in Valenhold’s Grand Clothiers’ Arcade. She outfitted the group, made tiny hero capes for Pip and Mela, recognized Jayson as something outside ordinary Vulpanii experience, and told him old stories concerning many-tailed fox spirits. |

There were also several **unnamed people directly encountered**, including Sari and Mela’s two original student attackers, vivarium captives and medical staff, Marshals, university staff involved in the seizure, Vaska/Fellan’s Guild associates, villagers from Sari’s home, shop workers, and the dangerous-animal handler from the earlier investigation.

One important distinction: **Elder Shaveth was not actually encountered in Chapter 14**. Veskara told Jayson about him and agreed to contact him.
